The program offers over 100 video lessons, access to a dedicated platform, and personalized support including one-on-one sessions, custom feedback, and access to CTO assistance. It covers various aspects of the software development process, including AI integration, marketing strategies, and operational management, allowing participants to manage their own SaaEs independently while enhancing productivity and reducing development costs. The training includes modules on building SaaCs, market research, and creating a business model, along with ongoing support and monitoring to ensure effective product launch.

A Flow is a drag-and-drop platform designed to help businesses create and manage their sales pipelines. It offers 14 integrated services that include website creation, email marketing, SMS marketing, appointment scheduling, and customer relationship management (CRM) tools. Users can easily build websites, forms, and automated calendars with customizable templates, automate customer interactions, and track lead generation through various dashboards and reports. The platform aims to streamline operations for small and medium-sized businesses by providing automation solutions tailored to their specific needs.
AI Sales Genio is a platform that enhances WhatsApp marketing and customer engagement through AI-powered interactions. It allows businesses to create personalized promotions, manage customer inquiries, and automate responses for lead tracking in real-time. Key features include a smart CRM system that organizes conversations, voice engagement with AI bots, and tools for enhancing sales strategies, all aimed at improving customer satisfaction and increasing conversions.
AI Setter is a digital communication platform that automates lead qualification, answering questions, and booking appointments for local businesses and online brands. It operates through multiple channels, including email, Instagram, and voice messaging, allowing users to choose who the AI replies to and how they respond. Key features include customizable scheduling, real-time transcripts, consent-first practices, and the ability to manage account information with control over permissions. The platform supports both inbound and outbound communications, ensuring a consistent approach to customer service.

partner intelligence platform (PIPE) is a cloud-based solution designed to enhance the discovery, qualification, and growth of B2B technology partners. It continuously profiles the partner ecosystem and provides real-time insights into its interactions with various companies, helping businesses identify and engage with the right partners effectively. Key features include automated partner search algorithms, detailed analysis of partner compatibility, technology expertise, and marketing alignment, all aimed at improving decision-making and revenue generation for enterprise partner programs. The platform also offers data governance services to ensure compliance and security in its operations.
